### [243] [Theory of criticality-enabled $U(1)$ symmetry breaking in a class of 1+1D systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38581)
*E. S. Andriyakhina,A. S. Shankar,T. Senthil,Z. D. Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出1+1D Lifshitz量子临界点自发U(1)对称破缺的解析理论，并用DMRG在 itinerant-fermion 链中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释并验证最近提出的1+1D Lifshitz量子临界点自发U(1)对称破缺现象，阐明其机制与后果。

Method: 构建含两个周期标量θ、φ的低能场论，其Berry相耦合使θ与U(1)电荷密度∂_xφ共轭；在大N极限下解析求解，并给出z≠1时支持U(1)长程序的解析论证；用有限尺寸与无限系统DMRG在具有U(1)⋊Z_2对称性的 itinerant-fermion 链上检验。

Result: 大N极限下，带电相位顶点e^{iβθ}呈长程序，而共轭顶点e^{iβφ}呈拉伸指数衰减，log⟨e^{iβφ(x)}e^{-iβφ(0)}⟩∝−|β|^{4/3}|x|^{2/3}；DMRG显示在Z_2铁磁转变处自旋扇区映射到Lifshitz场论，带电自旋-向列键算符S_i^+S_{i+1}^+呈长程序，电子Green函数呈拉伸指数衰减。

Conclusion: 证实临界性可导致1+1D中U(1)对称破缺，解析预测与数值结果一致。

Abstract: We present an analytic theory of the recently proposed phenomenon of spontaneous $U(1)$ symmetry breaking at 1+1D Lifshitz quantum critical points. The low-energy field theory contains two periodic scalars $θ$ and $φ$, with a Berry phase coupling that makes $θ$ canonically conjugate to the $U(1)$ charge density $\partial_x φ$. Within a controlled large-$N$ limit, we demonstrate that the $U(1)$-charged phase vertex $e^{iβθ}$ develops long-range order, while the conjugate vertex $e^{iβφ}$ decays as a stretched exponential, $\log\langle e^{iβφ(x)} e^{-iβφ(0)}\rangle\propto-|β|^{4/3}|x|^{2/3}$. Going beyond the large-$N$ limit, we give an analytic argument that the Lifshitz field theory supports $U(1)$ long-range order provided its dynamical exponent satisfies $z\neq1$, a condition strongly supported by existing calculations. We test these predictions using finite-size and infinite-system DMRG in an itinerant-fermion chain with $U(1) \rtimes \mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry. At the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ ferromagnetic transition, the spin sector of the chain maps to the Lifshitz field theory. Consistent with analytic predictions, the $U(1)$-charged spin-nematic bond operator $S_i^+S_{i+1}^+$ exhibits long-range order, while the electron Green's function shows stretched-exponential decay. Together, these results elucidate the mechanism and consequences of criticality-enabled $U(1)$ symmetry breaking in 1+1D.

</details>


### [244] [Sign problem and criticality in world-line quantum Monte Carlo methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38740)
*Rubem Mondaini,Richard T. Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了世界线量子蒙特卡洛中符号问题的温度与尺寸依赖，指出即使对自由费米子采样配分函数也会出现符号问题，并将其与非解析行为及硬核玻色子的有限温度KT相变联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于符号问题的讨论多集中于行列式量子蒙特卡洛，关注其作为量子多体模型求解瓶颈的作用，并已系统刻画其随空间格点尺寸、掺杂、温度和相互作用强度的行为；而世界线量子蒙特卡洛中符号问题的温度与尺寸依赖性研究相对薄弱，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者在世界线量子蒙特卡洛框架下分析符号问题，考察即使在采样自由费米子配分函数时符号问题的出现，并将其与对应参考模型——格点硬核玻色子——的非解析行为相联系；进一步研究该非解析行为如何印刻在二维紧束缚模型自由费米子权重的平均符号上。

Result: 格点硬核玻色子在二维中呈现有限温度Kosterlitz-Thouless相变，这种非解析行为被印刻到对应自由费米子二维紧束缚模型权重的平均符号之中，表明符号问题携带相变信息。

Conclusion: 即使直接符号采样已不可行，相变信息仍可能保留在符号问题之中，从而扩展了对符号问题物理意义的理解。

Abstract: Discussions of the sign problem usually focus on its role as the primary limitation of the applicability of quantum Monte Carlo methods for reliably solving quantum many-body models. Its behavior as a function of spatial lattice size, doping, temperature, and interaction strengths has been carefully characterized within the determinant quantum Monte Carlo algorithm. Here, we consider the much less well-explored question of the temperature- and size-dependence of the sign problem in the world-line quantum Monte Carlo method. We review how, in this algorithm, even sampling the partition function of free fermions yields a sign problem, which we connect to non-analytic behavior in the corresponding reference model, hard-core bosons on a lattice. The latter exhibits a finite-temperature Kosterlitz-Thouless phase transition in 2D, and we show how this non-analytic behavior is imprinted on the average sign of the weights for the corresponding free fermion 2D tight-binding model. Our results thus show how phase-transition information can remain encoded in the sign problem even after direct sign sampling becomes impractical.

</details>


### [245] [Quantum quenches of scar states in the Affleck-Kennedy-Lieb-Tasaki model via Clifford augmented tensor network simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38848)
*Azar C. Nakhl,Ben Harper,Muhammad Usman,Thomas Quella*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用qudit Clifford增强模拟方法研究AKLT模型中的magic，发现其基态与scar态SRE很高但纠缠低，可用低键维Clifford增强MPS表示并借助Clifford增强TDVP进行时间演化；在Haldane相内淬火至反铁磁Heisenberg哈密顿量后，string order发生衰减，且随bimagnon数增加更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford增强方法能利用稳定子结构高效模拟量子电路与多体系统，并便于量化non-stabilizerness（magic）；作者希望借此探测AKLT模型中magic的存在及其对动力学的影响。

Method: 采用qudit Clifford增强模拟方法，以Stabiliser Rényi Entropy（SRE）量化magic；将AKLT基态与scar态表示为低键维Clifford增强Matrix Product State；并使用Clifford增强time-dependent variational principle（TDVP）算法进行时间演化。

Result: AKLT模型的基态和scar态具有很高的magic（SRE高），但由于纠缠结构低，仍可写成低键维Clifford增强MPS并可时间演化。在Haldane相内淬火到反铁磁Heisenberg哈密顿量后，scar态的string order出现衰减，且随bimagnon数增加衰减更明显。

Conclusion: AKLT scar态的string order对保对称淬火敏感，即使初末哈密顿量属于同一拓扑相也不例外。

Abstract: Clifford augmented methods have emerged as a powerful technique to simulate quantum circuits and many-body systems by exploiting the stabiliser structure found in said systems, whilst additionally enabling one to readily quantify the non-stabilizerness (i.e.\ magic) present. In this work, we utilise a qudit Clifford augmented simulation method to probe the presence of magic in the Affleck-Kennedy-Lieb-Tasaki (AKLT) model, finding that its ground state and scar states are highly magical as quantified by their Stabiliser Rényi Entropy (SRE). Nevertheless, owing to its low entanglement structure, these states are still readily expressible as a Clifford augmented Matrix Product State with low bond-dimension and moreover may be time-evolved using the Clifford augmented time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) algorithm. We utilise Clifford augmented TDVP to study the string order dynamics in the scar states of the AKLT model after a quench within the Haldane phase to the anti-ferromagnetic Heisenberg Hamiltonian. We observe that there is a decay of string order following the quench, which is more pronounced with increasing bimagnon number. This finding highlights the sensitivity of string order in the AKLT scar states to symmetry-preserving quenches, even when the initial and final Hamiltonians belong to the same topological phase.

</details>


### [246] [Eigenstate thermalization at the edge of the many-body spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38973)
*Arnav Pushkar,Pranay Patil,Soumya Bera,Masaki Tezuka,Sumiran Pujari*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究不相容局域对称性对本征态热化（ETH）的支配作用，发现非可积性可与其共存，且ETH行为能深入多体谱边缘，使低温区仍具预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为大量相互兼容（对易）的对称性或守恒量会导致热化失效。本文关注：当存在大量相互不相容的局域对称性时，本征态热化将如何被支配，这一情形此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 选取一类键依赖的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 对称自旋-$\frac{1}{2}$ 量子哈密顿量，该模型天然具有不相容对称结构并导致谱中指数级大的简并；借助谱诊断手段分析非可积性及ETH行为。

Result: 当守恒量彼此不相容时，非可积性可在大范围守恒量存在下依然保持；同时ETH行为在该结构作用下能持续深入到多体谱边缘，即在低激发能密度下仍可获得足够的热力学熵。

Conclusion: 不相容局域对称性提供了一种机制，使ETH在近基态的低温区域依然可操作、可预测，突破了传统热化失效的认知。

Abstract: It is well-known that thermalization breaks down in presence of an extensive number of symmetries or conservation laws that are mutually compatible or commuting with each other. We ask here how an extensive number of mutually incompatible local symmetries govern eigenstate thermalization (ETH). We will investigate this through models within the class of bond-dependent $\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric quantum spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ Hamiltonians that naturally admit this structure. This leads to exponentially large degeneracies in the spectrum governed by the incompatible symmetry structure. Here, using spectral diagnostics, we show that non-integrability can survive despite extensively many conserved quantities when they are incompatible. Furthermore, we show that ETH behavior persists deep into the many-body spectral edge due to this structure. In other words, incompatible local symmetries enable a mechanism to obtain enough thermodynamic entropy at low excitation energy densities to make ETH operational or predictive near the ground state.

</details>


### [247] [Tunability of the structural and magnetic transition in kagome material: PrIr$_3$B$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39175)
*Gayathri V,Irshad K A,Sathishkumar M,Boby Joseph,S. Manni*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用同步辐射X射线粉末衍射研究了kagome晶格化合物PrIr₃B₂中与两步金属-绝缘体-金属（MIM）转变相关的异常结构相变，发现其具有温度和压力可调性，并伴随电荷有序和磁超晶格能隙等电子基态特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PrIr₃B₂作为kagome晶格金属磁体，其两步金属-绝缘体-金属转变及共存结构十分异常。作者希望厘清该结构转变的温度、压力响应，并关联其电阻率、磁性行为，以理解kagome体系中可能出现的电子有序态。

Method: 采用同步辐射X射线粉末衍射，在变温（环境压力）和变压（室温）条件下追踪PrIr₃B₂的结构演化；结合各向异性磁测量，绘制磁相图并分析超晶格能隙行为。

Result: 常压室温下，单斜（C2/m）与六方（P6/mmm）相以孪晶形式共存。降温至280 K（常压）或加压至1.2 GPa（室温）时完全转变为单斜相。单斜相晶格参数随温度呈现复杂演化，在约250 K和约110 K出现微弱但可辨识的异常，分别对应第二次MIM转变和电阻率的线性-非线性温变交叉。10 K下反铁磁转变附近打开超晶格能隙，即使在极化态下也不闭合。

Conclusion: 该两步MIM转变及其异常可能源于部分能隙化的费米面所对应的电荷有序态。PrIr₃B₂的晶体结构、磁性与电子基态具有温度和压力可调性，预示该kagome金属磁体存在有前景的电子有序相。

Abstract: We report the temperature and pressure tunability of an unusual structural transformation associated with a two-step metal-insulator-metal (MIM) transition in the kagome lattice compound PrIr$_3$B$_2$ using synchrotron X-ray powder diffraction. At ambient conditions of temperature and pressure, the monoclinic ($C2/m$) and the hexagonal ($P6/mmm$) phases coexist as twinned structure in the crystal. As the temperature (pressure) is decreased (increased), PrIr$_3$B$_2$ converts fully to monoclinic structure at $T =$ 280 K (at ambient pressure) and $P =$ 1.2 GPa (at room temperature). Temperature dependence of the monoclinic structure at ambient pressure presents complex evolution of lattice parameters with weak but clearly discernable anomalies at \SI{\sim 250} {\K} and \SI{\sim 110} {\K}, which are correlated with the second MIM transition and the linear to nonlinear temperature-dependent resistivity crossover, respectively. These anomalies are likely due to some charge order state causing a partially gapped Fermi surface. The magnetic phase diagram of PrIr$_3$B$_2$ is also investigated from anisotropic measurements. At 10 K, a superzone gap opens near the antiferromagnetic transition, which does not close even in the polarized state. From the tunability of the crystal structure and magnetic and electronic ground state, promising electronic orders are indicated in this kagome metallic magnet.

</details>


### [248] [Multipartite entanglement hidden in vector-chiral correlations of spin-1/2 chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39258)
*Tokuro Shimokawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文为周期性自旋-1/2链的总最近邻矢量手性建立了严格的纠缠判据：任意k-可分态的每自旋量子Fisher信息不超过k+1，超过该界即可认证纠缠深度至少为k+1，并在扩展J1-J2 XXZ链中验证了零温与有限温下的多体纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料中的纠缠表征是核心难题。已有方法通过单体自旋算符之和的量子Fisher信息将纠缠与可测磁响应联系，但推广到局域复合可观测量缺乏相应的纠缠界，限制了可探测的纠缠类型。

Method: 针对偶数格点周期性自旋-1/2链，推导总最近邻矢量手性的量子Fisher信息严格上界（每自旋≤k+1，对k-可分态成立）；结合无限密度矩阵重整化群(iDMRG)与热纯态量子态(TPQ)计算，在扩展J1-J2 XXZ链中数值研究矢量手性关联。

Result: 给出了复合可观测量情形下的严格纠缠判据，超过界即认证纠缠深度≥k+1；在扩展J1-J2 XXZ链中于零温和有限温下证实了编码于矢量手性关联中的多体纠缠。

Conclusion: 矢量手性与电极化之间的自旋流耦合，原则上可通过多铁磁体中的介电损耗谱获取该纠缠见证，将纠缠诊断从偶极自旋关联拓展到矢量手性类关联。

Abstract: Characterizing entanglement in quantum materials through experimentally accessible observables remains a central challenge. Quantum Fisher information associated with sums of one-site spin operators provides one route by linking entanglement to measurable magnetic response functions. Extending this approach to local composite observables requires new entanglement bounds. Here we establish a rigorous bound for the total nearest-neighbour vector chirality of periodic spin-$1/2$ chains with an even number of sites. The quantum Fisher information per spin cannot exceed $k+1$ for any $k$-producible state, so exceeding this bound certifies entanglement depth of at least $k+1$. Combining infinite density-matrix renormalization group and thermal pure quantum-state calculations, we demonstrate multipartite entanglement encoded in vector-chiral correlations at zero and finite temperatures in an extended $J_1$-$J_2$ XXZ chain. The spin-current coupling between vector chirality and electric polarization offers, in principle, access to this witness through dielectric loss spectroscopy in multiferroic magnets, extending entanglement diagnostics beyond dipolar spin correlations.

</details>


### [249] [Varying A-site radius and size disorder to tune magnetic ordering in compositionally complex perovskite oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39303)
*Madeleine Geers,Ravi Kiran Dokala,Roland Mathieu,Rebecca Clulow*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了在单一相固溶体中容纳十二种阳离子的钙钛矿氧化物AM7O3（A为五种稀土元素等摩尔混合，M为七种过渡金属等摩尔混合）的合成，其与A=La、Gd及La1/2Gd1/2的对照样品均在89–115 K呈亚铁磁有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁化学的长期目标是通过成分工程调控磁有序温度，因此需要在单相中引入多阳离子以实现对磁性的连续调节。

Method: 合成单一相钙钛矿氧化物固溶体，A位由La、Sm、Gd、Nd、Dy五种稀土等摩尔组成，M位由Ti、Cr、Mn、Fe、Co、Ni、Cu七种过渡金属等摩尔组成；对A5M7O3及A=La、Gd、La1/2Gd1/2样品进行块体磁测量。

Result: 所有化合物均在89–115 K之间表现为亚铁磁有序；磁性受尺寸无序参数、A位阳离子半径与价电子数的共同影响。

Conclusion: 通过多阳离子成分工程可调控钙钛矿氧化物的磁有序温度，尺寸无序参数与A位半径、价电子数的组合是理解其磁性的关键。

Abstract: A long standing goal of magnetochemists is to be able to control ordering temperatures through compositional engineering. Here we report the synthesis of a perovskite oxide with twelve cations accommodated within a single phase solid solution, AM7O$_3$, A = La$_{\frac{1}{5}}$Sm$_{\frac{1}{5}}$Gd$_{\frac{1}{5}}$Nd$_{\frac{1}{5}}$Dy$_{\frac{1}{5}}$ (A5), and M7 = Ti$_{\frac{1}{7}}$Cr$_{\frac{1}{7}}$Mn$_{\frac{1}{7}}$Fe$_{\frac{1}{7}}$Co$_{\frac{1}{7}}$Ni$_{\frac{1}{7}}$Cu$_{\frac{1}{7}}$. Bulk magnetometry measurements for A5M7O$_3$, as well as for A = La, Gd and La$_{\frac{1}{2}}$Gd$_{\frac{1}{2}}$, show that all these compounds magnetically order as ferrimagnets between 89 and 115 K. We find that the magnetic properties are influenced by considering a combination of the size disorder parameter alongside the A-site cationic radii and valence electrons.

</details>


### [250] [Weyl superconductivity from Feshbach resonance in the three-dimensional repulsive Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39418)
*Taekyoung Kim,Pit Bermes,Giorgia A. Busin,Fabian Grusdt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文理论上研究了三维费米-哈伯德模型的超导相，通过构建三维Feshbach机制给出强耦合下由掺杂近共振束缚态驱动的统一微观图像，预言一种时间反演对称破缺的d_{x²-y²}+id_{z²}配对态，并估计其临界温度及无能隙Weyl点特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受三维费米-哈伯德模型中反铁磁相变首次实验实现的推动，作者希望进一步理解该模型的三维超导相，为量子模拟实验与大规模数值计算提供可检验的理论预言。

Method: 将Feshbach机制推广到三维，构建强耦合下的统一微观配对图像：配对由掺杂粒子的近共振束缚态驱动；分析这些长寿命束缚态在三维中与二维 qualitatively 不同的内部结构，从而推导出相应的配对对称性。

Result: 三维中束缚态内部结构的差异导致形成一种独特的超导态，即时间反演对称破缺的d_{x²-y²}+id_{z²}配对态；给出了相应临界温度的估计，并提供证据表明该超导相存在无能隙Weyl点。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子模拟领域树立新的里程碑，其预言可被实验与大规模数值方法检验。

Abstract: Motivated by the first experimental realization of the antiferromagnetic phase transition in the three-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard model, we present a theoretical study of the model's 3D superconducting phase. By formulating a 3D extension of the Feshbach mechanism, we provide a unified microscopic picture at strong coupling in which pairing is driven by near-resonant bound states of dopants. These long-lived bound states acquire a qualitatively different internal structure in three dimensions compared to their two-dimensional counterparts, giving rise to a distinct superconducting state, namely a time-reversal symmetry breaking $d_{x^2-y^2}+id_{z^2}$ pairing state. We also provide an estimate of the corresponding critical temperature, and provide evidence that the resulting superconducting phase hosts gapless Weyl points. Our results represent a new milestone for the field of quantum simulation, challenging experiments and large-scale numerics alike to test our predictions.

</details>


### [251] [Chiral quantum chaos around exponentially many zero modes in the quantum breakdown model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39404)
*Kohei Kawabata,Kinya Guan,Hosho Katsura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在零维全连通量子击穿模型中建立对称性与量子混沌的分类，揭示其Z4周期性、五类手性对称类、指数多体零模及能隙分离与硬边谱统计特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子击穿模型源于电介质击穿物理，由随机相互作用费米子构成。作者希望理解其零维全连通版本中的对称性结构与量子混沌行为，并将其与Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev模型的对称性分类进行类比。

Method: 研究零维、全对全相互作用的量子击穿模型，对费米子模式数进行对称性分类，分析手性对称类的实现、手性指数保护的多体零模、谱隙的分离与标度，以及对称性增强的硬边谱统计。

Result: 该模型关于费米子模式数呈现Z4周期性，类似于SYK模型的对称性分类；实现了全部五类手性对称类；存在指数数量、由手性指数保护的多体零模；给出谱隙的分离与标度规律，并观察到作为手性对称类量子混沌标志的对称性增强硬边谱统计。

Conclusion: 零维全连通量子击穿模型具有丰富的对称性与量子混沌结构，手性对称类中的多体零模和硬边谱统计为其独特特征，为随机相互作用费米子系统的谱分类与混沌研究提供了新范例。

Abstract: The quantum breakdown model is a model of randomly interacting fermions, motivated by the physics of dielectric breakdown. Here, we establish classification of symmetry and quantum chaos in a zero-dimensional, all-to-all-interacting version of the quantum breakdown model. It exhibits the $\mathbb{Z}_4$ periodicity with respect to the number of fermionic modes, reminiscent of the symmetry classification of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model. A unique feature of the quantum breakdown model is that it realizes all the five classes with chiral symmetry and hosts an exponentially large number of many-body zero modes protected by the chiral index. We elucidate the separation and scaling of the spectral gap and demonstrate the symmetry-enriched hard-edge spectral statistics as signatures of quantum chaos in the chiral symmetry classes.

</details>


### [252] [Qantum Well Resonant Tunneling Diode Probe of Correlated States in Twisted Bilayer MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39417)
*Chengjie Zhou,Jin-Xin Hu,Sihong Xu,Zijing Jin,Hui Li,Kam Tuen Law,Jiannong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文开发了量子阱共振隧穿二极管（QWRTD）新实验平台，用于探测约57°扭转双层MoS₂（tMoS₂）的电子结构，观察到ν=1的整数关联绝缘态及位移场诱导的ν=3/4分数绝缘态，并将其表征为可能的广义Wigner晶体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属硫族化合物（TMD）中的莫尔超晶格为探索强电子关联驱动的涌现量子相提供了理想平台，但需要在近AB堆叠等特殊构型下获得直接的关联态实验证据。作者希望开发新的探测手段，以研究此类体系中的强关联与拓扑性质。

Method: 构建量子阱共振隧穿二极管（QWRTD）作为实验平台，对约57°扭转双层MoS₂测量微分电导dI/dV_Probe随填充因子ν和位移场D的变化；并进行温度和磁场依赖测量，同时在另一扭转角约56.5°的器件中重复验证。

Result: 在ν=1处观察到稳健的整数关联绝缘态，且在所测位移场范围内持续存在；在D < -75 mV/nm时发现位移场诱导的ν=3/4分数绝缘态，温度与磁场依赖测量表明其可能是广义Wigner晶体；这些关联态在另一扭转角约56.5°的器件中同样被观测到。

Conclusion: 结果为近AB堆叠tMoS₂中的关联态提供了直接证据，并确立了QWRTD作为研究范德华异质结中强关联与拓扑性质的有力实验工具。

Abstract: Moiré superlattices formed in transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) offer a versatile platform for exploring emergent quantum phases arising from strong electronic correlations. In this work, we develop a new experimental platform, the quantum well resonant tunneling diode (QWRTD), to probe the electronic landscape of $\approx 57^\circ$ twisted bilayer MoS$_2$ (tMoS$_2$). By measuring the differential conductance ($dI/dV_{\text{Probe}}$) as a function of filling factor $ν$ and displacement field $D$, we observe a robust integer correlated insulating state at $ν= 1$ that persists across the measured displacement field range. Furthermore, we identify a displacement-field-induced fractional insulating state at $ν= 3/4$ for $D < -75$~mV/nm. Temperature- and magnetic-field-dependent measurements characterize this $ν= 3/4$ state as a potential generalized Wigner crystal. These correlated states are also observed in another device with a similar twist angle ($\approx 56.5^\circ$). Our results provide direct evidence of correlated states in near-AB-stacked tMoS$_2$ and establish QWRTD as a powerful experimental tool for investigating strong correlations and topology in van der Waals heterostructures.

</details>


### [253] [Designing Strongly Correlated Quantum Phases of Matter with Foundation Neural-Network Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40209)
*Alessandro Sinibaldi,Luciano Loris Viteritti,Riccardo Rende,Federico Becca,Giuseppe Carleo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于基础神经网络量子态的从头算逆向设计框架，通过耦合空间梯度优化寻找目标基态性质，在阻挫海森堡模型中发现了新的量子自旋液体候选模型 $J_1$-$J_2$-$\delta$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料逆向设计需寻找使基态具备目标性质的微观哈密顿量耦合，而强关联量子系统高效求解此问题仍基本不可行。作者希望构建通用框架，用于搜索阻挫海森堡模型的非磁相并识别新的量子自旋液体候选。

Method: 以基础神经网络量子态（Foundation Neural-Network Quantum States）为核心，将一族哈密顿量的基态编码于单一变分波函数中。由于拟设显式依赖耦合，任意目标性质对耦合可微，哈密顿量搜索化为耦合空间的梯度优化。应用于正方晶格阻挫海森堡模型，逐步增加自由次近邻耦合数目：单个耦合、两个对角耦合独立变化、$2\times2$ 元胞内八个独立耦合。

Result: 单耦合情形回复正方 $J_1$-$J_2$ 海森堡模型已知的非磁窗口；两对角耦合独立变化时，发现连接正方晶格与各向异性三角晶格区域的扩展非磁区。八独立耦合搜索空间中，优化自发收敛到 $J_1$-$J_2$-$\delta$ 海森堡模型，即对角耦合在相邻格板上交替取两值，此模型近期在交变磁性语境中被提出。对该最优模型进行至 $16\times16$ 集团的有限尺寸标度，表明基态无磁序、二聚序或格板序。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了 $J_1$-$J_2$-$\delta$ 模型的基态为一种新的量子自旋液体候选，区别于此前在正方晶格上提出的量子自旋液体，同时验证了基础神经网络量子态框架在强关联量子体系从头算逆向设计中的有效性。

Abstract: Designing a material with a desired property amounts to solving an inverse problem: finding the couplings of a microscopic Hamiltonian whose ground state exhibits that property. For strongly correlated quantum systems, solving this problem efficiently remains largely out of reach. To address this challenge, we present a general framework for ab initio inverse design based on Foundation Neural-Network Quantum States, a recent approach in which the ground states of a family of Hamiltonians are encoded in a single variational wave function. Because the ansatz depends explicitly on the couplings, any target property is a differentiable function of them, and the search for the right Hamiltonian reduces to gradient-based optimization in coupling space. We apply this approach to search for nonmagnetic phases of frustrated Heisenberg models on the square lattice with an increasing number of free next-nearest-neighbor couplings, aiming to identify new quantum spin liquid candidates. With a single coupling, the method recovers the known nonmagnetic window of the square $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model, whereas letting the two diagonal couplings vary independently reveals an extended nonmagnetic region connecting the square-lattice and anisotropic-triangular-lattice regimes. In a search space of eight independent couplings within a $2\times2$ unit cell, which contains several paradigmatic frustrated spin models, the optimization spontaneously converges to the $J_1$-$J_2$-$δ$ Heisenberg model, in which the diagonal couplings alternate between two values on neighboring plaquettes, a model recently proposed in the context of altermagnetism. Finite-size scaling up to $16\times16$ clusters in this optimal model shows that the ground state has no magnetic, dimer, or plaquette order, establishing it as a new quantum spin liquid candidate, distinct from those previously proposed on the square lattice.

</details>


### [254] [$μ$SR measurements of 3D Ising ferromagnetism in bulk Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$ crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39806)
*M. N. Wilson,J. Huang,T. J. Hicken,D. A. Mayoh,B. M. Huddart,G. Balakrishnan,T. Lancaster*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过磁强计和μSR测量表明，Fe₃GeTe₂在2 K至居里温度220 K的整个温区内均为三维Ising型铁磁性，170 K处的异常并非铁磁-反铁磁转变，而是磁畴冻结转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe₃GeTe₂是具有范德华层状结构的准二维巡游铁磁体，具有显著磁各向异性，其薄样品中铁磁矩的可栅极调控性引起广泛关注。然而该材料在低温下还存在一个尚未定论的磁状态，部分研究认为随温度降低会发生铁磁到反铁磁的转变，这一争议需要澄清。

Method: 采用磁强计测量（magnetometry）与μ子自旋弛豫（μSR）技术对Fe₃GeTe₂的磁性进行表征。

Result: 数据显示Fe₃GeTe₂在T_C=220 K至2 K的整个温度范围内均表现为三维Ising型铁磁性；此前被归因于反铁磁转变的170 K特征，实际很可能对应磁畴冻结转变。

Conclusion: Fe₃GeTe₂在整个低温区间内保持三维Ising型铁磁序，不存在铁磁-反铁磁转变；170 K异常应重新解释为畴冻结现象，这修正了以往对该材料低温磁性的认识。

Abstract: Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$ is a quasi-two-dimensional itinerant ferromagnet with a van-der-Waals layered structure that exhibits substantial magnetic anisotropy. In bulk samples, this material becomes magnetically ordered below 220~K, while in thin samples, the gate-tunability of the ferromagnetic moment has attracted significant interest. Alongside high-temperature ferromagnetism, a low-temperature magnetic state occurs whose nature has not been conclusively identified, with some studies suggesting a transition between ferromagnetism and antiferromagnetism with temperature. Here we present magnetometry and $μ$SR data that demonstrate that Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$ exhibits three-dimenional Ising-like ferromagnetism over the entire temperature range between $T_{C}=220$~K and 2~K. The feature at 170~K that has previously been proposed to be a transition to antiferromagnetism is instead likely to represent a domain-freezing transition.

</details>


### [255] [Strong Dimerization and Field-Induced Reconstruction of the Low-Energy Spectrum in $\mathrm{Cu}_3(\mathrm{OH})_4(\mathrm{HCO}_2)_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39966)
*Sk Saniur Rahaman,S. R. Hassan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用分区块超块对角化方法(SBDM)结合弱耦合层的转移矩阵处理，研究了畸变三角量子反铁磁体Cu3(OH)4(HCO2)2的场依赖低温热力学，揭示了1/3磁化平台与低温热力学非单调行为的共同微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畸变三角量子反铁磁体中的1/3磁化平台及其低温热力学起源尚不清晰，需要从微观层面理解强二聚体交换层级如何缩减活跃磁希尔伯特空间并主导场驱动的残余自旋重整。

Method: 采用分区块超块对角化方法(SBDM)，配合弱耦合层的转移矩阵处理。利用以Cu1-Cu1二聚体内耦合J2=150 K为主的强交换层级，将24格点团簇划分为16个强束缚Cu1自旋与8个磁活性Cu2自旋两个扇区，分别进行热力学计算。

Result: 8个Cu2自旋极化给出Sz=4，相对饱和值Sz_sat=12，直接给出1/3磁化标度的微观来源。有限温热力学显示低能标度随场非单调演化：主导C/T特征随场增强先软化，在约2 T附近达最小值，随后随低温磁化接近M_sat/3而硬化。强还原的熵反映二聚体激发能标下热活性自由度受限。

Conclusion: 强二聚体诱导的活跃磁希尔伯特空间缩减，以及随后场驱动的残余自旋扇区重组，是1/3磁响应与低温热力学非单调行为的共同微观起源。

Abstract: We investigate the field-dependent low-energy thermodynamics of the distorted triangular quantum antiferromagnet $\mathrm{Cu}_3(\mathrm{OH})_4(\mathrm{HCO}_2)_2$ using a sector-resolved Superblock Diagonalization Method (SBDM) supplemented by a transfer-matrix treatment of weakly coupled layers. The strong exchange hierarchy, dominated by the Cu1--Cu1 intradimer coupling $J_2=150\,\mathrm{K}$, separates a high-energy dimer sector from a much softer magnetic manifold formed predominantly by the Cu2 moments. In the 24-site cluster, sixteen Cu1 spins form the strongly bound sector while eight Cu2 spins remain magnetically active; polarization of these eight spins gives $S^z=4$ compared with $S^z_{\rm sat}=12$, providing a direct microscopic origin for the one-third magnetization scale. The finite-temperature thermodynamics reveals a non-monotonic field evolution of the low-energy scale: the dominant $C/T$ feature softens with increasing field, reaches a minimum near the field region around $2\,\mathrm{T}$, and subsequently hardens as the low-temperature magnetization approaches $M_{\rm sat}/3$. Temperature and field sweeps thus expose a common field-induced spectral reconstruction, while the strongly reduced entropy reflects the restricted number of thermally active degrees of freedom below the dimer excitation scale. Our results identify strong-dimer-induced reduction of the active magnetic Hilbert space, followed by field-driven reorganization of the residual spin sector, as the common microscopic origin of the one-third magnetic response and the non-monotonic low-temperature thermodynamics.

</details>


### [256] [Entropy-Driven Altermagnetism from Thermal Magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40002)
*Tanaya Halder,Ashis K. Nandy,Anamitra Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由熵（升温）驱动的交替磁性（altermagnetism）产生机制，并在最小方格反铁磁体中予以验证：交换调制会劈裂磁振子谱，提高磁振子熵、降低自由能，从而在有限温度下稳定交替磁相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁性通常由晶格对称性/交换几何在零温下确定，缺乏对温度作为调控参量、特别是升温诱导交替磁性的机制研究。作者希望探索熵驱动的有序状态，为补偿磁体中热诱导交替磁性提供路径。

Method: 在最小方格反铁磁模型上引入交换调制，解析推导该调制态的不稳定性判据；结合变分自旋波理论、自旋-晶格蒙特卡洛和团簇对角化数值方法验证熵驱动不稳定性及其与磁序的共存。

Result: 交换调制因磁振子动量相关劈裂而熵增、自由能降低而被热力学青睐；得到不稳定性判据，预测温度驱动的重入行为；该调制态与Néel序的重叠区域构成有限温度交替磁相，并支持横向自旋电导。

Conclusion: 在耦合到足够软物理模式的补偿磁体中，可实现热诱导的交替磁性，提供了一条熵驱动交替磁性的通用路径。

Abstract: We identify a route to altermagnetism driven by entropy upon heating and demonstrate it in a minimal square-lattice antiferromagnet. An exchange modulation is entropically favored because the resulting momentum-dependent magnon splitting increases the magnon entropy, thereby lowering the free energy and favoring altermagnetism. We derive an instability criterion for this exchange-modulated state, predict its temperature-driven reentrant behavior, and show that its overlap with Néel order defines a finite-temperature altermagnetic phase. Variational spin-wave theory, spin--lattice Monte Carlo, and cluster diagonalization establish the entropy-driven instability and its coexistence with magnetic order. The resulting state supports a transverse spin conductivity. The mechanism provides a route to thermally induced altermagnetism in compensated magnets coupled to sufficiently soft physical modes.

</details>


### [257] [Impurity-induced Friedel oscillations and Wigner crystallization in Luttinger liquids via Unified Field Bosonization Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40053)
*Soundarya P,Venkata Suryanarayana M,Joy Prakash Das*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于统一场玻色化技术（UFBT），精确处理局域杂质、非微扰处理相互作用，得到强非均匀一维Luttinger液体中杂质诱导密度关联的解析表达式，并揭示g=1/3处的交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法多对杂质作微扰处理，难以刻画强非均匀体系中杂质与相互作用共同导致的密度关联。本文旨在建立一个杂质精确、相互作用非微扰的解析框架。

Method: 采用统一场玻色化技术（UFBT），在自由费米子层面精确纳入局域杂质，而非微扰地处理相互作用；推导费米子格林函数与密度-密度关联函数的闭式解析表达式；发展反射耦合谐振分析以处理杂质背散射引起的左右混合；并将无自旋情形作为有自旋形式的直接约化。

Result: 获得强非均匀体系下杂质诱导的2k_F Friedel振荡与4k_F Wigner晶体型关联的统一描述；解析导出标度行为，发现在g=1/3处主导的长程关联发生交叉：g>1/3时Friedel关联占优，g<1/3（强排斥区）时Wigner晶体型关联占优。

Conclusion: 该工作为强非均匀一维量子体系中杂质诱导与相互作用驱动的密度关联提供了可解析控制的统一框架。

Abstract: We present an analytical study of impurity-induced density correlations in interacting one-dimensional Luttinger liquids using the Unified Field Bosonization Technique (UFBT). Unlike conventional approaches that treat impurities perturbatively, UFBT incorporates localized impurities exactly at the free-fermion level while treating interactions non-perturbatively. Closed analytical expressions for fermionic Green functions and density-density correlation functions are obtained for strongly inhomogeneous systems, enabling a unified description of the impurity-induced $2k_F$ Friedel oscillations and the $4k_F$ Wigner-crystal like correlations. The corresponding scaling behaviour is derived analytically, revealing a crossover in the dominant long-distance correlations at $g=1/3$: Friedel correlations dominate for $g>1/3$, whereas Wigner-crystal like correlations become dominant for $g<1/3$, corresponding to the regime of sufficiently strong repulsive interactions. A reflection-coupled harmonic analysis is developed to account for the left-right mixing induced by impurity backscattering and to consistently reproduce the resulting density correlations. The spinless limit is also obtained as a direct reduction of the spinful formulation. These results provide an analytically controlled framework for describing impurity-induced and interaction-driven density correlations in strongly inhomogeneous one-dimensional quantum systems.

</details>


### [258] [Probing Collective and Individual Kondo Screening: Multi-Stage, Multi-Channel Kondo Effects in a $C_3$-Symmetric Four-Impurity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40109)
*Danqing Hu,Jiangfan Wang,Zixiang Hu,Yi-feng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过设计四杂质Kondo模型，利用辅助浴数值重整化群方法，区分了多杂质Kondo体系中的集体屏蔽与个体屏蔽，并揭示了二者分别导致的多阶段双通道Kondo效应与不稳定的三通道Kondo效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体屏蔽被认为是多杂质与晶格Kondo体系基本物理的核心机制，但如何确立这一图像并将其与个体（局域）Kondo屏蔽区分开来仍是重大挑战。作者希望借助新发展的数值方法，为这一长期争议提供可判别的物理判据。

Method: 基于近期发展的辅助浴数值重整化群（AUNRG）方法，作者在C_3对称的三杂质Kondo模型基础上，设计了一个四杂质Kondo（4IK）模型：其中探测杂质仅与原有三个Kondo杂质耦合，用以探测被屏蔽态的性质，并比较集体屏蔽与个体屏蔽所对应的通道结构。

Result: 结果表明，集体Kondo屏蔽产生涌现的多阶段、双通道Kondo效应，而个体（局域）屏蔽则导致不稳定的三通道Kondo效应。这暗示被屏蔽态具有特殊的螺旋结构，从而证实了多杂质Kondo体系中集体屏蔽的思想；同时表明辅助浴构造可方便地推广到含更多杂质的模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为区分集体与个体Kondo屏蔽提供了明确的判据，验证了多杂质Kondo体系中集体屏蔽的图像，并展示了AUNRG方法在探索具有涌现集团自由度的新奇多体量子态方面的可扩展性。

Abstract: Collective screening has been proposed to underlie the basic physics of multi-impurity and lattice Kondo systems. But how to establish this picture and distinguish it from individual (local) Kondo screening remains a grand challenge. The recently developed auxiliary-bath numerical renormalization group (AUNRG) method provides a key step towards resolving this issue. Its application to the $C_3$-symmetric three-impurity Kondo (3IK) model with a shared electron bath reveals fully screened Fermi liquid ground states arising from collective screening of cluster spin degrees of freedom at small Kondo coupling $J_{\rm K}$ and individual (local) screening of local impurities at large $J_{\rm K}$. Here we design a four-impurity Kondo (4IK) model where the probe impurity couples only to the original three Kondo impurities to detect the nature of the screened states. We show that the collective and individual Kondo screenings give rise to emergent multi-stage, two-channel Kondo effect and unstable three-channel Kondo effect, respectively. This suggests a special helicity structure of the screened states and confirms the idea of collective screening in multi-impurity Kondo systems. Our work also demonstrates that the same auxiliary-bath construction can be readily extended to models with additional impurities to explore novel many-body quantum states with emergent cluster degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [259] [adapol: Adaptive pole-fitting for quantum many-body physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40150)
*Zhen Huang,Chia-Nan Yeh,Lin Lin,Nils Wentzell,Jason Kaye,Hugo U. R. Strand*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 我们介绍了 adapol，一个用于通过紧凑的极点展开拟合松原格林函数、自能和杂化函数的 Python 软件包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多个物理与数值场景中都需要将松原格林函数等表示为简单极点求和，如精确对角化量子杂质求解器的杂化拟合、微扰论、张量网络、离散 Lehmann 表示压缩、离散极点图示方法及解析延拓。现有方法在精度、紧凑性或噪声鲁棒性方面存在不足，需要一个黑箱式且稳健的极点拟合工具。

Method: adapol 采用改进的 AAA 有理逼近算法估计极点位置，再通过非凸优化精修极点位置，从而使拟合同时具备高精度和紧凑性，并具有黑箱和噪声鲁棒的优点。它提供简洁自包含的接口、详尽文档与示例，并包含与 TRIQS 的接口。

Result: 该方法能够以紧凑的简单极点求和（实极点位置与矩阵值留数）准确拟合松原格林函数、自能和杂化函数，并在黑箱和含噪情况下保持稳健。软件包提供完善文档、示例以及 TRIQS 接口。

Conclusion: adapol 为松原函数的极点展开拟合提供了一个准确、紧凑、黑箱且噪声鲁棒的工具，适用于多种量子多体计算场景，并易于集成到现有工作流中。

Abstract: We describe adapol, a Python package for fitting Matsubara Green's functions, self-energies, and hybridization functions by a compact sum of simple poles with real pole locations and matrix-valued residues. This pole-fitting step arises in many contexts, including hybridization fitting for quantum impurity solvers based on exact diagonalization, perturbation theory, and tensor networks, the compression of existing pole expansions such as the discrete Lehmann representation, diagrammatic methods based on discrete pole representations, and the analytic continuation of Matsubara Green's functions. adapol uses a modified version of the AAA rational approximation algorithm to estimate pole locations, and non-convex optimization to refine them. This procedure yields accurate and compact fits in a black-box and noise-robust manner. adapol provides a simple, self-contained interface with extensive documentation and examples, as well as an interface to the TRIQS package.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Hidden Angular Momentum Loop Currents in Symmetric Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38317)
*Rule Yi,Vincent P. Flynn,Benedetta Flebus*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出环形电流可起源于晶格本身，由量子与热涨落维持；在保持时间反演、反演和四重旋转对称性的二维弹性晶格中，相邻格胞出现相反电流环，而每个格点的平均角动量为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上环形电流序被用于解释非常规电子相，而晶格通常被视为被动的背景宿主。作者希望探究环形电流是否可以不依赖电子或磁序，而直接源于晶格动力学本身。

Method: 建立角动量交换的微观描述，将格点间角动量转移与晶格回复力矩分离；在保持时间反演、反演和四重旋转对称性的二维弹性晶格模型中分析声子涨落所诱导的定向弹性相互作用与局域对称性降低环境。

Result: 在二维弹性晶格中发现相邻格胞上存在方向相反的电流环，同时每个格点的平均角动量为零；这些电流由声子真空零点涨落维持，并在有限温度下持续存在，其起源为局域对称性降低环境中的定向弹性相互作用，高对称性将电流组织为补偿式环流图样。

Conclusion: 研究将平衡态环形电流物理拓展至晶格动力学，表明弹性几何结构可将涨落组织为持续的角动量环流，无需电子序、磁序、外部驱动或自发对称性破缺。

Abstract: Loop-current order has been invoked to explain unconventional electronic phases, with the crystal lattice usually regarded as a passive host for the underlying collective dynamics. Here we show that circulating currents can originate in the lattice itself, sustained by quantum and thermal fluctuations. We develop a microscopic description of angular momentum exchange that separates transfer between sites from crystalline restoring torques and reveals how circulation can emerge in a symmetry-preserving equilibrium state. In a two-dimensional elastic lattice preserving time-reversal, inversion, and fourfold rotational symmetries, we uncover opposite current loops on neighboring plaquettes while the mean angular momentum vanishes at every site. These currents are sustained by zero-point fluctuations in the phonon vacuum and persist at finite temperature. We trace their origin to directional elastic interactions in locally symmetry-lowered environments, with the higher crystal symmetry organizing the resulting currents into a compensated pattern of circulation. Our results extend the physics of equilibrium loop currents to lattice dynamics, revealing that elastic geometry can organize fluctuations into persistent angular momentum circulation without electronic or magnetic order, external driving, or spontaneous symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [261] [Three-Dimensional Shankar Skyrmions in Frustrated Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38624)
*Vladyslav M. Kuchkin,Ricardo Rama-Eiroa,Carlos Saji,Alvaro S. Nunez,Roberto E. Troncoso*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文构建了阻挫手性反铁磁体中三维Shankar skyrmion的连续介质理论，导出了亚稳态有限尺寸π₃(SO(3))孤子的形成条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三维非阿贝尔拓扑织构（Shankar skyrmion）在反铁磁体系中的存在性与稳定机制，并寻找其微观实现路径。

Method: 采用连续介质理论，结合Derrick–Hobart标度分析、两种微观构造（非共线反铁磁的旋转框架序与幅度软化Néel场的S³扩展），并进行数值最小化和有效动力学分析。

Result: 交换作用、Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用、各向异性与阻挫可相互平衡以确定有限平衡尺寸；数值最小化得到亚稳态单极织构，有效动力学识别出相干呼吸振荡为其特征有限频率集体模式。

Conclusion: 提出了Shankar skyrmion静态与动力学的微观理论框架，指明阻挫手性反铁磁体是三维非阿贝尔拓扑织构的潜在宿主材料。

Abstract: We formulate a continuum theory for three-dimensional (3D) Shankar skyrmions in frustrated chiral antiferromagnets (AFs) and derive the conditions for metastable finite-size $π_3(SO(3))$ solitons. A Derrick--Hobart scaling analysis shows that exchange, Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction (DMI), anisotropy, and frustration can balance to set a finite equilibrium size. We identify two microscopic routes to this common topological and stabilization framework: intrinsic rotation-frame order in noncollinear AFs and an $\mathbb{S}^3$ extension of an amplitude-softened Néel field. In the latter, the smooth four-component texture projects onto a Néel field containing a spatially separated pair of oppositely charged Bloch points. Numerical minimization yields metastable monopole textures, while the effective dynamics identifies coherent breathing oscillations as their characteristic finite-frequency collective mode. Our results provide a microscopic framework for the statics and dynamics of Shankar skyrmions and identify frustrated chiral AFs as promising hosts of 3D non-Abelian topological textures.

</details>


### [262] [Optical observation of interlayer spin correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38825)
*Akiyoshi Park,Pranshoo Upadhyay,Andrey Grankin,Emil Viñas Boström,Mahdi Ghafariasl,Hassan Alnatah,Masoud Mohammadi-Arzanagh,Gautam Nambiar,Beini Gao,Alireza Alvandi,Sakthi Rajmano Madhan Kumar,Ghadah Alshalan,Isaac Sherwood,Mahmoud Jalali Mehrabad,You Zhou,Arun Ramanathan,Xavier Roy,Angel Rubio,Mohammad Hafezi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文首次实现层间自旋关联的直接光学探测：在范德华反铁磁体CrSBr中利用双磁振子拉曼散射，结果与无拟合参数的微观自旋波模型高度吻合；该通道仅在反铁磁态开启、磁场驱动至铁磁态时消失，并可通过激子共振增强约十倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层间自旋关联决定层状量子材料的磁基态与跨界面自旋输运，是二维磁性的基本量，但此前难以直接测量，实验上一直无法获取。

Method: 采用双磁振子拉曼散射对范德华反铁磁体CrSBr进行光学探测，并与微观自旋波模型对比；同时发展激子介导的变体技术，将激光调至激子共振附近以增强信号。

Result: 双磁振子信号与无拟合参数的自旋波模型高度一致；该通道仅在反铁磁态出现，磁场使晶体转变为铁磁态时消失；激子共振附近信号增强约十倍，源于激子的大振子强度。

Conclusion: 磁振子对拉曼光谱为范德华磁体的层间自旋关联提供了直接光学窗口，并可推广至扭转双层与近邻耦合异质结构。

Abstract: Spin correlations govern numerous collective behaviors of quantum materials, underpinning exotic phenomena such as unconventional superconductivity and topological magnetism. In layered materials, the interlayer spin correlation is important because it characterizes the magnetic ground state and determines spin transport across the interface. Yet interlayer spin correlations have remained hard to measure directly, leaving one of the most basic quantities of two-dimensional magnetism out of experimental reach. Here we provide the first direct optical probe of interlayer spin correlations, using two-magnon Raman scattering in the van der Waals antiferromagnet (AFM) CrSBr, in remarkable agreement with a microscopic spin-wave model without any fitting parameter. Moreover, the two-magnon channel switches on only in the AFM state and vanishes when a magnetic field takes the crystal to a ferromagnetic state. We furthermore establish an exciton-mediated variant of the technique, where tuning the laser near the exciton resonance enhances the signal roughly tenfold due to the exciton's large oscillator strength. Magnon-pair Raman spectroscopy thus opens a direct optical window into interlayer spin correlations in van der Waals magnets, extendable to twisted bilayers and proximity-coupled heterostructures.

</details>


### [263] [Spin Birefringence and Spin Polarized Transmission across p-wave Altermagnetic Heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39174)
*Yong-Mei Zhang,Zhi-Ping Niu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于Hamiltonian构建波函数与线性方程组，研究了N/AM界面及N/AM/N异质结中的电子输运，得到了反射/透射系数与自旋极化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自旋选择性输出和自旋电子学器件的实验研究奠定理论基础。

Method: 基于三个区域的Hamiltonian提出一般波函数，构建联立线性方程组，求解反射系数、透射系数及自旋极化率。

Result: 电子从N入射到AM时散射分裂为+支与−支，具有不同折射角，+支可能发生全反射，临界角由入射条件和AM材料性质决定；N/AM/N异质结中自旋向上/向下电子传播方向相同但透射概率不同，透射与极化随结长周期变化；交换场和自旋劈裂强度对自旋极化透射率影响显著，自旋轨道耦合影响很小。

Conclusion: 通过调节AM材料参数可优化系统，为自旋选择性输出和自旋电子学器件提供理论依据。

Abstract: This paper investigates electron transport properties across N/AM interface or N/AM/N heterojunction. Based on Hamiltonians in the three regions, general wave functions are proposed and combined linear equations are constructed. Reflection and transmission coefficients as well as spin polarizatioin of the altermagnet heterostructure are obtained. When electron incident from normal metal to altermagnet, it is scattered and split into + branch and _ branch transmitting into altermagnet zone with separate refractive angles. Total internal reflection might happen to + branch. Critical angle of total reflection is determined by incident conditions and properties of AM material. In the heterostructure case, spin-up and spin-down electrons transmitted with same propagation directions but different transmission probabilities. Transmission and polarization periodically change with length of the junction. Exchange field and spin-splitting strength have pronounced effect on spin-polarized transmittance while the effect of spin-orbit coupling is quite small. Optimized system can be found by tuning parameters of the AM material. This study will lay the theoretical groundwork for experimental research on spin-selective outputs and spintronic devices.

</details>


### [264] [Giant PhotoMagnetoDiode Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39435)
*V. L. Korenev,S. A. Tarasenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文报道了光磁二极管效应，在0.1 T磁场下获得高达10的整流比，光电流对电压呈二极管式依赖，光致发光中不对称性更强。现象可由电场与磁场中载流子双极漂移加快速表面复合理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半导体中实现磁控的非互易输运（二极管式整流）是自旋电子学与光电子学的关注点，而此前在低磁场下获得巨大整流比的光磁二极管效应尚缺乏明确的实验观测与统一解释。

Method: 采用高质量VPE生长的GaAs样品，在低温下进行带间光激发，施加外磁场并测量光电流随偏压极性的变化以及光致发光的不对称性；同时用电磁场中载流子双极漂移结合快速表面复合的理论进行建模对比。

Result: 在0.1 T磁场下观测到巨大的整流比约10；光电流呈现二极管式电压依赖，且光导在同时反转电场和磁场极性时保持不变；光致发光中观察到更强的不对称比；实验数据与双极漂移加快速表面复合理论吻合良好。

Conclusion: 光磁二极管效应源于电场与磁场共同作用下的载流子双极漂移与快速表面复合，可在极低磁场下产生显著非互易输运，为磁控光电器件提供机制基础。

Abstract: We report the observation of a photomagnetodiode effect with a giant rectification ratio of 10 at a magnetic field of 0.1 T. The effect consists of a diode-like dependence of the photocurrent on the applied voltage that emerges in an external magnetic field. Such a pronounced nonreciprocity is observed in high-quality VPE-grown GaAs samples at low temperatures at interband photoexcitation. The photoconductivity is invariant upon reversing the polarity of both electric and magnetic fields. Similar behavior with even higher asymmetry ratio is observed in photoluminescence. The findings are well described by the theory of ambipolar drift of carriers in electric and magnetic fields together with fast surface recombination.

</details>


### [265] [Emergent Quantum Geometric Phases in Holey Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39500)
*Pablo Canteli,Yuriko Baba,Juan Salvador-Sánchez,Jorge Estrada-Álvarez,Ana Pérez-Rodríguez,Carlos Sánchez-Sánchez,Vito Clericò,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Francisco Domínguez-Adame,Rafael A Molina,Enrique Diez,Elena Díaz,Mario Amado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在超高质量图形化反点阵封装单层石墨烯中进行磁输运测量，揭示了由经典回旋轨道与量子磁通匹配共同主导的层次化可公度性现象，并证明无需扭转或堆叠即可在单层石墨烯中几何生成类莫尔电子谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯等二维材料中电子密度的周期性调制可显著改变能谱与输运性质，但受限于样品质量与特征长度尺度，可控研究可公度性与超晶格现象存在困难。作者希望建立一种清洁、可调、可扩展的平台，以按需的实空间设计来探索莫尔物理。

Method: 采用封装单层石墨烯结合超高精度光刻图形化的周期性反点阵，保持材料本征电子性质；通过系统调节点阵尺寸进行磁输运测量，分析经典回旋轨道主导的可公度特征与量子磁通-单胞匹配引起的Brown-Zak振荡，并测量本征几何相位。

Result: 观察到由具有可比半径、沿多条经典轨迹运动的回旋轨道产生的层次化经典可公度特征，表现为超出单轨道图像的展宽电阻峰；在其上叠加有源自磁通量子与Bravais点阵单胞量子可公度性的显著Brown-Zak振荡；本征几何相位可直接测量；磁场周期性与光刻周期图形精确匹配。

Conclusion: 纳米图形化石墨烯可作为清洁、可调、可扩展的平台，通过按需实空间设计在单层石墨烯中几何生成类莫尔电子谱，无需扭转、晶格失配或多层堆叠，从而为探索莫尔物理提供新途径。

Abstract: In graphene and other two-dimensional materials, periodic modulations of the electron density can significantly alter the energy spectrum and transport properties. Here, we report magnetotransport measurements in encapsulated monolayer graphene with ultra-high-quality patterned periodic antidot lattices that preserve the intrinsic electronic properties of the material. This lithographically defined platform enables controlled access to commensurability and superlattice phenomena at length scales otherwise difficult to achieve. By systematically tuning the lattice dimensions, we reveal a hierarchy of classical commensurability features arising from cyclotron orbits with comparable radii that follow multiple classical trajectories, resulting in broadened resistance peaks beyond the conventional single-orbit picture. Superimposed on these features, we observe pronounced Brown-Zak oscillations arising from the quantum commensurability between the magnetic flux quantum and the unit cell of the engineered Bravais lattices. We demonstrate that the intrinsic geometric phase of our system is directly measurable and show a precise matching of the magnetic field periodicity to the lithographic periodic patterning, where moiré-like electronic spectra can be geometrically generated in single-layer graphene without the need for twist, lattice mismatch, or multilayer stacking. Our results establish nanopatterned graphene as a clean, tunable, and scalable platform for realizing and exploring moiré physics through on-demand real-space design

</details>


### [266] [Anisotropic wavevector-dependent damping of thickness-quantized magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39612)
*Tamara Azevedo,Rostyslav O. Serha,Yannik Kunz,Matthias R. Schweizer,Vitaliy I. Vasyuchka,Mathias Weiler,Andrii V. Chumak,Burkard Hillebrands,Mikhail Kostylev,Alexander A. Serga*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用高分辨参量不稳定光谱探测微米厚YIG薄膜中厚度量子化自旋波，揭示阻尼的各向异性波矢依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有描述多模磁振子系统阻尼时多假设为模式无关的有效参数，掩盖了依赖传播几何与模式剖面的波长相关弛豫过程。

Method: 采用高分辨率参量不稳定光谱，探测微米厚钇铁石榴石（YIG）薄膜中波长低至约百纳米的厚度量子化自旋波；并将结果与偶极-交换理论对比。

Result: 不稳定阈值随磁场呈规则锯齿形依赖，源于离散厚度模式之间的切换；对比理论揭示出各向异性的波矢相关阻尼，其随模式数增大，且对平面内与平面外波矢分量依赖不同。

Conclusion: 磁振子阻尼并非模式无关，而是具有各向异性的波矢依赖性，这对自旋波传输与多模非线性动力学的准确描述至关重要。

Abstract: Magnon damping is a key factor governing spin-wave transport and nonlinear dynamics of multimode magnon systems. However, many descriptions rely on the assumption of an effective mode-independent parameter, which can mask wavelength-dependent relaxation processes that depend on propagation geometry and mode profile. Here, we employ high-resolution parametric-instability spectroscopy to probe thickness-quantized spin waves with wavelengths down to about a hundred nanometers in micrometer-thick yttrium iron garnet films. The instability threshold exhibits a regular sawtooth dependence on the magnetic field, arising from switching between discrete thickness modes. Comparison with dipole--exchange theory reveals anisotropic wavevector-dependent damping that increases with mode number and depends differently on the in-plane and out-of-plane wavevector components.

</details>


### [267] [Magnetization relaxation of interacting chains of nanomagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39811)
*Denis Ledue,Renaud Patte,Francois Vernay,Hamid Kachkachi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究一维偶极耦合纳米磁体链中从单粒子到集体磁化动力学的过渡，结合IHD解析法与TQMC模拟，给出弛豫速率闭合表达式与双指数弛豫曲线，并揭示场控的均匀反转到边缘成核传播的过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解偶极耦合纳米磁体阵列中单粒子行为向集体行为的过渡机制，并寻求计算高效的弛豫动力学预测方法。

Method: 基于Langer理论的中间至高阻尼（IHD）解析方法，结合时间量化蒙特卡罗（TQMC）模拟，推导并验证弛豫速率与磁化弛豫曲线的半解析表达式。

Result: 得到含弱偶极相互作用的弛豫速率闭合表达式；给出相互作用链磁化动力学m(t)的双指数半解析公式；与TQMC模拟在宽参数范围内吻合良好；发现由偶极稳定化的空间不均匀性驱动的场控过渡：从均匀（类宏观自旋）反转转变为边缘成核传播。

Conclusion: 所推导表达式为偶极耦合纳米磁体组件的弛豫行为预测提供了计算高效框架。

Abstract: We investigate the magnetization dynamics crossover from single-particle to collective behavior in a one-dimensional chain of dipolar-coupled nanomagnets with uniaxial anisotropy. Using both an intermediate-to-high damping (IHD) analytical approach based on Langer's theory and time-quantified Monte Carlo (TQMC) simulations, we derive and validate semi-analytical expressions for the relaxation rate and the magnetization relaxation curves. Our main results include: (i) a closed-form expression for the relaxation rate accounting for (weak) dipolar interactions, (ii) a two-exponential semi-analytical formula for the magnetization dynamics $m(t)$ of an interacting chain, and (iii) a systematic comparison with TQMC simulations, showing good agreement for a wide range of parameters. The analysis reveals a field-controlled crossover from uniform (macrospin-like) reversal to edge-nucleation propagation, driven by the spatial inhomogeneity of dipolar stabilization. The derived expressions provide a computationally efficient framework for predicting the relaxation behavior of dipolar-coupled nanomagnetic assemblies.

</details>


### [268] [Multi-Branch Transport in a Back-gated WS$_2$ Transistor at Deep-Cryogenic Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39878)
*Megan Powell,Vilas Patil,Hazel Neill,Stephen O'Sullivan,Paul K. Hurley,Lida Ansari,Farzan Gity,Alessandro Rossi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究了背栅多层WS₂晶体管在低至20 mK的深低温下的电学性能，器件保持强栅控能力，开关比超过10⁵，并观察到可重复的"肩状"开启特性，用多有效导电支路并联的唯象模型予以描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料（尤其过渡金属硫族化合物）有望突破传统半导体技术的工作极限，其中WS₂在低温（深低温）工作方面尤为有前景，但多层WS₂晶体管在极低温下的电学行为与输运机制尚待深入表征与解释。

Method: 制备背栅结构的多层WS₂场效应晶体管，在深低温（低至20 mK）下进行电学测量，考察其栅极调控能力、开关比及开启特性；针对低温柔开启曲线中的可重复"肩状"特征，建立由多个有效导电支路并联构成的唯象模型进行拟合解释。

Result: 在整个低温区间器件均保持强栅可调性，有效开/关电流比超过10⁵；低温开启特性呈现可重复的肩状特征，可由多个并联有效导电支路的唯象模型描述。

Conclusion: 多层WS₂晶体管在深低温（20 mK）下仍具良好开关性能，是低温电子学应用的潜在候选；其异常开启特性提示存在多条并行导电通道，多支路唯象模型为理解低温输运机制提供了框架。

Abstract: Two-dimensional materials are promising candidates for electronic applications beyond the operating limits of conventional semiconductor technologies. Within this class, transition-metal dichalcogenides offer attractive properties for field-effect transistor operation, with tungsten disulphide (WS$_2$) emerging as a particularly promising material for operation at cryogenic temperatures. Here, we investigate the electrical performance of a back-gated multilayer WS$_2$ transistor at deep cryogenic temperature, down to 20 mK. The device remains strongly gate-tunable throughout the cryogenic regime, with an effective on/off current ratio exceeding $10^{5}$. Most notably, the low-temperature turn-on characteristics exhibit reproducible shoulder-like features, which we describe using a phenomenological model comprising multiple effective conduction branches operating in parallel.

</details>


### [269] [Thermal magnon transport in FM/AFM bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39947)
*Moumita Kundu,Ulrich Nowak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 理论上研究了铁磁/反铁磁双层中磁振子的传播，通过自旋塞贝克效应产生非平衡磁振子积累，发现磁振子模式的手性约束以及反铁磁体中热激发的自旋流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息处理依赖自旋电子学，磁态是数据存储与传输的高效载体。需要理解铁磁与反铁磁异质结构中磁振子的传播规律，以设计手性选择性自旋输运的异质结构。

Method: 构建铁磁/反铁磁双层模型，引入空间变化的温度分布来探测自旋塞贝克效应，产生局部磁振子激发与由热到冷的连续磁振子流，并通过非平衡磁振子积累对其进行量化；基于磁振子模式的手性分析传播约束。

Result: 识别出磁振子从铁磁进入反铁磁或反向传播时的特定手性约束；观察到反铁磁体中热激发的自旋流，而该现象在满足时间反演对称性的块体反铁磁体中通常不存在。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计用于磁振子手性选择性自旋输运的异质结构提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Progress in information processing relies on spintronics, where magnetic states serve as efficient carriers for data storage and transfer. In this work, we theoretically study magnon propagation in a bilayer composed of a ferro-magnet and an antiferromagnet. For this purpose, we probe the spin Seebeck effect by introducing a spatially varying temperature profile. This generates a local magnon excitation and a continuous magnon flux from hot to cold regions which we quantify through the resulting non-equilibrium magnon accumulation. Based on the chirality of these modes, we identify specific constraints for magnon modes traveling either from the ferromagnet into the antiferromagnet or vice versa. A key finding is the observation of a thermally triggered spin current in the antiferromagnet, a phenomenon typically absent in bulk antiferromagnets that obey time-reversal symmetry. These results provide important insights into the design of heterostructures for magnonic chirality-selective spin transport.

</details>


### [270] [Atomic-Scale Imaging of Lattice Relaxation and Topological Flat Bands in Helical Trilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39986)
*Shudan Jiang,Zonglin Li,Yu Gu,Liang Liu,Dandan Guan,Yaoyi Li,Hao Zheng,Canhua Liu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Shengwei Jiang,Xiaoxue Liu,Zhiwen Shi,Guorui Chen,Jinfeng Jia,Tingxin Li,Can Li,Shiyong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用近场光学显微与低温扫描隧道显微技术对螺旋三层石墨烯（HTG）进行实空间研究，直接观测到超莫尔晶格弛豫形成的三角畴与条纹畴、蜂窝状平带织构及畴壁一维边界态，并确定魔角约为1.9°，显著大于理论预测的1.6°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTG作为可高度调控的莫尔量子材料，具备强电子关联与非平庸能带拓扑，但其原子尺度晶格结构与局域电子性质此前基本未被实验探索，缺乏晶格弛豫与平带之间的直接微观联系。

Method: 结合扫描近场光学显微镜与低温扫描隧道显微镜，对HTG进行实空间表征；通过原子级分辨谱学测量探测平带与边界态，并系统改变扭转角以确定魔角位置。

Result: 直接成像超莫尔晶格弛豫，观察到由锐利畴壁分隔的大三角畴以及由平滑渐变边界连接的条纹畴；原子尺度谱学揭示具有蜂窝电子织构的平带和受限于畴壁的一维边界态；系统变角测量给出魔角约1.9°，明显大于理论预言的1.6°。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了HTG中晶格弛豫与平带之间的直接微观联系，表明实际魔角受晶格弛豫等效应影响而偏离理论值，为莫尔量子材料的能带工程与关联物态调控提供了实空间实验依据。

Abstract: Helical trilayer graphene (HTG) has emerged as a highly tunable moire quantum material that hosts strong electronic correlations and nontrivial band topology. However, the atomic-scale lattice structure and local electronic properties have remained largely unexplored. Here we present a comprehensive real-space study of HTG using a combination of scanning near-field optical microscopy and low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy. We directly image supermoire lattice relaxation, revealing large triangular domains separated by sharp domain walls, as well as stripe domains connected by smoothly varying boundaries. Atomic-scale spectroscopy uncovers flat bands with a honeycomb electronic texture and one-dimensional boundary states confined to domain walls. By systematically varying the twist angle, we identify a magic angle of approximately 1.9°, substantially larger than the 1.6 degree predicted by theory. Our results establish a direct microscopic link between lattice relaxation and flat bands in HTG.

</details>


### [271] [Fingerprints of unconventional pairing in superconductor-hole-gas heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40059)
*Serafim S. Babkin,Karsten Flensberg,Jeroen Danon,Maksym Serbyn*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出通过面内磁场下超流刚度与自旋磁化率的非解析行为，以及刚度张量场角依赖的傅里叶谐波（可经动电感测量获取），来识别超导体/二维空穴气异质结中诱导的配对能隙结构与非常规配对及立方Rashba耦合特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导体与二维空穴气异质结是量子器件的潜在平台，但其本征性质（如非常规配对）难以通过实验直接探测，亟需可实验获取的探针来表征诱导能隙结构。

Method: 理论分析面内磁场下超流刚度与自旋磁化率的磁场依赖关系，考察其非解析性；并分析刚度张量随磁场角度的依赖，提取其傅里叶谐波，结合动电感测量作为实验手段。

Result: 超流刚度和自旋磁化率的面内磁场依赖呈现非解析性，编码了空穴气中诱导的能隙结构；刚度张量的场角依赖傅里叶谐波可作为非常规配对与立方Rashba耦合的指纹特征，且该谐波可通过动电感测量获得。

Conclusion: 面内磁场响应（超流刚度、自旋磁化率的非解析性及刚度张量角依赖的傅里叶谐波）可作为探测超导体/二维空穴气异质结中诱导能隙结构与非常规配对的有效实验指纹。

Abstract: Superconductor and two-dimensional hole gas heterostructures are promising platforms for quantum devices, but their intrinsic properties, e.g., unconventional pairings, are hard to access experimentally. Here, we show that the in-plane-field dependence of the superfluid stiffness and spin susceptibility exhibits nonanalyticities encoding the induced gap structure in the hole gas. Additionally, Fourier harmonics of the stiffness tensor's field-angle dependence, accessible via kinetic-inductance measurements, provide fingerprints of unconventional pairing and cubic Rashba coupling.

</details>


### [272] [Topological Transverse Transport without a Gap in Critical Topological Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40065)
*Chang-geun Oh,Tomoki Ozawa,Jun-Won Rhim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了临界拓扑平带（CTFB）在低温下本征电霍尔、热电霍尔与热霍尔响应的理论，揭示平带宏观简并导致的化学势奇异漂移会产生代数-对数型低温修正（T ln(1/T)、T[ln(1/T)]²、T[ln(1/T)]³），并论证体谱隙对零温霍尔量子化非必需、但对指数级热保护不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子霍尔输运依赖体谱隙来隔离占据子空间并指数抑制热涨落；新发现的临界拓扑平带虽具有精确平带与整数陈数，却与色散连续谱相交。其有限温度横向输运性质此前完全未被研究，因此需要建立相应的低温输运理论。

Method: 构建CTFB中本征电霍尔、热电霍尔与热霍尔响应的低温理论；采用固定粒子数与固定化学势（钉扎到平带能量）两种约束情形进行分析，借助Lambert-W函数描述化学势漂移。

Result: 固定粒子数时，平带宏观简并迫使化学势发生奇异的Lambert-W漂移，产生代数-对数层次修正：电霍尔为T ln(1/T)，热电霍尔为T[ln(1/T)]²，热霍尔为T[ln(1/T)]³，取代了有隙陈绝缘体的激活型热保护。固定化学势时，平带在任意非零温下被锁定在半占据，阻碍T→0⁺时完全填充拓扑基态的恢复。

Conclusion: 体谱隙并非零温霍尔量子化的必要条件，但对霍尔量子化的指数级热保护是必需的。

Abstract: Quantized Hall transport is traditionally anchored to a bulk spectral gap, which isolates the occupied subspace and exponentially suppresses thermal deviations. Recently discovered critical topological flat bands (CTFBs) challenge this paradigm: an exactly flat band touches a dispersive continuum while retaining a well-defined integer Chern number. However, their finite-temperature transverse transport properties remain entirely unexplored. Here, we develop a low-temperature theory of the intrinsic electrical, thermoelectric, and thermal Hall responses in CTFBs. At fixed particle number, the macroscopic flat-band degeneracy forces a singular Lambert-$W$ drift of the chemical potential, generating an algebraic-logarithmic hierarchy of low-temperature corrections: $T\ln(1/T)$ for electrical Hall, $T[\ln(1/T)]^2$ for thermoelectric Hall, and $T[\ln(1/T)]^3$ for thermal Hall conductivity, replacing the activated thermal protection of a gapped Chern insulator. By contrast, externally pinning the chemical potential to the flat-band energy locks the flat band to half occupation at any nonzero temperature, obstructing the recovery of the fully filled topological ground state as $T\to0^+$. Our results establish that a bulk spectral gap is unnecessary for zero-temperature Hall quantization, but indispensable for its exponential thermal protection.

</details>


### [273] [Long-lived divergence from equilibrium of electrons, nuclear spins and lattice for a solid state ion trap at low temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40268)
*Guy Matmon,Manuel Grimm,Markus Mueller,Byron J. Villis,Andrew J. Fisher,Gabriel Aeppli*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过光学方法建立Ho³⁺:LiYF₄的电子-核能级方案，发现低温下不同实验协议产生显著不同的非平衡态，核自旋激发无需声子协助即可扩散并平衡，且倾向于获得比电子自旋更高的有效温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡化是物理与工程中的基本问题，而不同子系统（电子、核自旋、声子）各自及相互如何平衡尚不清楚。能级交叉在耦合自由度动力学中具有特殊作用，因为纠缠可在此被最大化以加速弛豫，因此需要研究可调量子涨落模型系统中的平衡过程。

Method: 采用光学方法确定包含回避交叉与非回避交叉的电子-核能级结构；以稀土离子Ho³⁺掺杂于LiYF₄盐中作为模型系统，通过横向于四方晶格长轴的外磁场调节量子涨落；以扫描纵向场为变量监测各能级布居，追踪系统状态。

Result: 低温下，依据实验协议的不同会出现差异极大的非平衡态。证据表明核自旋激发可在无声子协助下扩散并平衡，且倾向于获得比电子自旋更高的有效温度，而晶格温度更低。热辅助隧穿理论排除了标准的声子辅助隧穿情景，提示能级反交叉处的快速动力学完全由核自旋扩散促成。

Conclusion: 该工作为稠密、多组分相互作用量子系统中的热化及相关慢弛豫现象提供了新的理解，揭示核自旋扩散可作为能级反交叉处快速动力学的独立机制。

Abstract: A fundamental problem in physics as well as engineering is equilibration. For example, the regulation of thermal and quantum fluctuations enables thermal and quantum annealing of complex systems. A key question is how different subsystems, such as electrons, nuclear spins and phonons equilibrate on their own as well as with each other. Level crossings play a special role in the dynamics of coupled degrees of freedom, for it is here that entanglement can be maximized to speed up relaxation. Here we use optical methods to establish the electronuclear level scheme, including avoided and unavoided crossings, and to examine equilibration of a rare earth ion (Ho$^{3+}$) in a salt (LiYF$_4$), a model system with quantum fluctuations which can be tuned via an external magnetic field transverse to the crystallographic long axis of the tetragonal host. We track the state of the system by monitoring the populations of the levels as a function of swept longitudinal fields, and discover that at low temperatures, depending on the experimental protocol, vastly different non-equilibrium states arise. We find evidence that nuclear spin excitations diffuse and equilibrate without the assistance of phonons, and tend to acquire higher effective temperatures than the electronic spins and the yet cooler lattice. A theory of thermally assisted tunneling rules out the standard scenario of phonon -assisted tunneling, and instead suggests that fast dynamics at level anti-crossings are facilitated entirely by nuclear spin diffusion. This provides a new understanding of thermalization and related slow relaxation phenomena in dense, multi-component interacting quantum systems.

</details>


### [274] [Quantization through Dissipation and the Optical Quantum Hall Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40337)
*Zhenisbek Tagay,Ahmed Abouelkomsan,Yugo Onishi,Adbhut Gupta,Loren Pfeiffer,Liang Fu,N. P. Armitage*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过高精度太赫兹法拉第旋转测量与数值计算，提出整数量子霍尔效应量子化的一种新机制：仅回旋共振无法给出量子化平台，必须纳入杂质态在低频处拓扑强制要求的微弱耗散贡献，DC霍尔电导才被恢复为量子化值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数量子霍尔效应的霍尔电导精确量子化为 e²/h 的整数倍，已有解释包括 Laughlin 规范论证（规范不变性）、边缘通道图像（手性弹道一维通道）与体态拓扑不变量论证。这些解释各自捕捉不同侧面，但缺乏从体态有限频率耗散电动力学出发的统一动力学理解，作者旨在补充这一新路径。

Method: 采用高精度太赫兹法拉第旋转实验测量，结合数值计算，对体态耗散电动力学进行分析；重点考察回旋共振在 Kramers-Kronig 变换下的贡献，并检验加入杂质态低频耗散项后的效果。

Result: 单独的回旋共振在 Kramers-Kronig 变换下不能产生量子化平台；只有纳入杂质态提供的微弱、低频、拓扑强制要求的耗散贡献后，量子化才得以恢复。该耗散项虽微弱，却在动力学上把 DC 霍尔电导与有限频率光学响应联系起来。

Conclusion: 量子化可由体态有限频率耗散电动力学机制解释：一个隐藏在耗散响应中的动力学机制将 DC 值与有限频率光学响应绑定，为通过光学霍尔效应理解量子化开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The integer quantum Hall effect remains the most paradigmatic and remarkable example of exact quantization in condensed matter physics. Its Hall conductivity $σ_{xy}$ is fixed to $e^2/h$ times an integer to a precision limited only by measurement and independent of disorder or interactions. This robustness has several explanations, each capturing a strikingly different piece of the physics. In Laughlin's gauge argument, flux insertion pumps an integer charge between edges, so quantization follows from gauge invariance alone. The edge-channel picture instead attributes transport to chiral, ballistic 1D channels at the sample boundary, one per filled Landau level. Bulk arguments tie $σ_{xy}$ to a topological invariant of the disordered system, with extended states compensating exactly for the current not carried by localized ones. Here we demonstrate an additional route to understanding quantization, rooted in the finite-frequency dissipative electrodynamics of the bulk. Using high-precision terahertz Faraday rotation and numerics, we show that the cyclotron resonance alone does not give quantized plateaus under Kramers-Kronig transformation. Quantization is recovered only once a faint, low-frequency, topologically enforced dissipative contribution from impurity states is included. This dynamical mechanism, hiding in plain sight within the dissipative response, ties the DC value to the finite-frequency optical response and offers a new route to quantization through the optical Hall effect.

</details>


### [275] [Resonances control when multiterminal Josephson currents reduce to two-terminal couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40346)
*A. Barış Özgüler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文检验多端子约瑟夫森结中"两两耦合网络"描述的准确性，发现其误差由费米能级附近耦合到三个及以上端子的正常区模式控制，通过栅极失谐该模式可将误差从数十百分比降至1%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多端子约瑟夫森结被视为拓扑Andreev能带、多对超电流和可调电路元件的载体，其电流既可建模为两两三端子耦合网络，也包含真正的多端子过程（如四重态）。作者希望回答：两两描述究竟有多准确，以及什么因素控制其误差。

Method: 在平面结的微观散射模型下，将精确电流与最优两两近似（允许任意非正弦耦合）进行对比。研究对象包括无序三端子和四端子结系综、设计的多达十六端子的器件，以及两个清洁器件的有限能隙计算。此外构建解析单能级模型确定任意端子数下实现两两精度所需的失谐量。

Result: 在两两近似下，能量变化几乎被完全捕捉，但电流误差中位数达8.0%至21.2%，约为能量误差的两倍，因为电流对多端子谐波加权更强。误差机制为：费米能级附近耦合到三个及以上端子的正常区模式产生大的非两两电流，用栅极使其失谐可抑制该电流。仅依据正常态性质选出的该模式，可在不拟合电流的情况下预测八个三、四端子器件的栅极依赖误差。清洁器件的有限能隙计算确认误差从共振附近的数十百分比降至1%以下。解析单能级模型给出任意端子数下实现两两精度所需的充分失谐条件。

Conclusion: 平面结继承了量子点中已知的共振与共隧穿区制，且正常态性质可识别在两者之间进行选择的模式。这表明多端子结的两两描述精度由单一共振模式控制，可通过栅极调节实现高精度近似。

Abstract: Multiterminal Josephson junctions, in which one weak link couples three or more superconductors, are studied as hosts of topological Andreev bands, multi-pair supercurrents and tunable circuit elements. Their currents are modeled both as pairwise networks of two-terminal couplings and through genuinely multiterminal processes such as quartets. We ask how accurate the pairwise description is and what controls its error. In a microscopic scattering model of planar junctions, we compare exact currents with the best pairwise approximation, which allows arbitrary nonsinusoidal couplings. In ensembles of disordered three- and four-terminal junctions, the pairwise terms capture nearly all of the energy variation, yet the median current error ranges from 8.0 to 21.2 percent, about twice the energy error, because currents weigh the multiterminal harmonics more strongly. Designed devices extend the comparison to sixteen terminals. We identify a mechanism that controls this error. A normal-region mode near the Fermi level that couples to three or more terminals produces large nonpairwise currents, and detuning it with a gate suppresses them. Shifting only this mode, selected from normal-state properties, predicts the gate dependence of the error in eight three- and four-terminal devices without fitting currents. Finite-gap calculations in two clean devices confirm a drop from tens of percent near resonance to below one percent. An analytic single-level model gives a sufficient detuning for pairwise accuracy at any terminal count. Planar junctions thus inherit the resonant and cotunneling regimes known from quantum dots, and normal-state properties identify the mode that selects between them.

</details>
